- **tidb-vector-index-restrictions-tiflash-1bda0c67e19cb960**: TiDB vector index restrictions: TiFlash required, cosine and L2 only. Use when hitting this exact issue with TiDB vector index restrictions. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tidb-vector-index-restrictions-tiflash-1bda0c67e19cb960))
- **tidb-vector-index-show-warnings-explai-f7d49da7a897e917**: TiDB vector index: SHOW WARNINGS explains why the ANN index was not used. ordering by the wrong distance function gives the warning ANN index not used: not ordering by COSINE distance. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tidb-vector-index-show-warnings-explai-f7d49da7a897e917))
- **tidb-vector-search-ann-index-not-used-fb58ef1c81445884**: TiDB Vector Search: ANN index not used - EXPLAIN shows warnings. the two classic causes: ordering by the wrong distance function (the index is built on one function, for example cosine, and the query orders by L2), and using DESC order when the index only supports ascending. Use when hitting this exact issue with TiDB Vector Search. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tidb-vector-search-ann-index-not-used-fb58ef1c81445884))
- **tidb-vector-search-error-1105-vector-h-d3b1c77c31ef76da**: TiDB Vector Search: ERROR 1105 vector has N dimensions, does not fit VECTOR(M). Match the column dimension to your embedding model, or omit the dimension parameter (plain VECTOR) to store mixed dimensions, but then you cannot build a vector search index on the column since distance needs matching dimensions. Use when hitting this exact issue with TiDB Vector Search. Not for unrelated errors or different features. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tidb-vector-search-error-1105-vector-h-d3b1c77c31ef76da))
